- **A07-microstructure-feeds**: All evidence collected. The `onBook` emits `{bids, asks}` (not `topBids/topAsks`), and consumers expect `bids/asks` — so the naming is consistent, the brief's "emits bids/asks not topBids/topAsks" concern is a non-issue (consumers consume `bids/asks` correctly). - **a11y**: An accessibility prober for this monorepo's Vue surfaces. Given a scope and a depth, it reads templates the way a screen-reader / keyboard-only user would — running the eslint-a11y rules, sniffing for ARIA-without-keyboard smells, and (deep) booting a fixture to run @axe-core/playwright — and returns structured, severity-rated findings.
